# A025 stage 5: non-flat three-core data release counterfamily

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Full FEI remains OPEN. Uniform actual Boolean signs, bit entropy and
ordinary physical reach weights throughout. Advance the existing C024/
C019 owner interface beyond the constant-failure flattening of stage4.
The tested false rule is: at every mixed DATA query, actual entropy
decrement D_i is at most K Inf_i plus lambda times the release of the
fixed symmetric boundary bank R_star, for absolute finite K,lambda>=0,
without an additional inherited child/terminal surplus. This is a local
constant-price rule, not a universal policy or an indexed source theorem.

For every even m>=4, take one quadratic bent seed on m data bits, a shared
balancing literal z, a fair data bit x, and two distinct edits at vertices
0 and e0 conditional on x=0. These are three distinct nonconstant balanced
cores, selected by two fair nested point blocks with original terminal
weights(1/2,1/4,1/4). At the query of x before either controller resolves,
PROOF.md proves, with delta=2^-m,

    d=delta/2, release=3delta/4=3d/2,
    B_x/d>=3/2+m/4,
    (D_x-lambda release)/d>=m/4+(3/2)(1-lambda).

For every finite K,lambda choose an even m>4K+6(lambda-1). Thus the
specified local payment fails for all finite constants. For K=4,lambda=1,
m=18 is already a rigorous actual Boolean witness.

Activating all three cores under the same fresh width-m point event keeps
the ratios, yields dimension2m+4, minority probability2^(-m-1) and total
influence tending to zero. All terminal entropy is retained. The original
family has H<3I, and the rare family H<6I; neither is an FEI counterexample.
The conserved bank and its remaining capacity remain valid and positive.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

PROOF.md supplies the complete all-m/all-activation-width construction.
Its new conversion to the conserved bank is exact: at each edited vertex
one label of probability1/4 is x-pivotal. Hence d=delta/2 and release=
2 E q(1-q)=3delta/4. The root bank is(3m+2)delta/4; after the query its
physical expected remainder is(3m-1)delta/4. All remaining directions and
both actual children are included. Common activation adds a V-direction
bank capacity to both histories; that capacity has ZERO x-release and
is explicitly preserved in the annotated output.

For empty controller frequency, half the original data Fourier labels
have eta=+1 and row coefficients u=sqrt(delta)*sigma-delta/2,
v=-delta/2. Every row has total mass>=delta/4 and derivative mass
delta^2/4, so its true sector entropy is at least m delta^2/4. The
nonempty controller sectors have equal sector masses and contribute
exactly3delta/4. This proves B_x/d>=3/2+m/4; no marginal h2(d) or
floating-point sign is substituted. JS>=0 transfers the bound to the
actual decrement. The exact rational witness m18 has lower margin
B_x-4d-release>=delta/4>0.

The source bank is independently reconstructed in REVIEW_A029.md at its
finite-label conservation scope. The edge-variance identity, fair
controller release and removed-data-direction release are accepted;
sharp compression, entropy sufficient conditions and earlier bundled
claims are excluded. No substantive repair is supplied and no new source
claim ID is invented. This source review precedes the narrow downstream
counterfamily; no broad universal candidate is opened at the review cap.

The rare extension scales both true B and actual entropy decrement by
the physical activation probability, because every original nonzero
Fourier row contains z and both actual x children are balanced. Its bank
release and query influence scale by the same probability. Unqueried
point directions' bank capacities are retained, not assumed absent.

First unsupported universal line: allocate one genuinely unused certified
child/terminal surplus to the dimension-growing mixed x residual, while
keeping the fixed remaining bank and both actual child bills, or select
a paid query that avoids the residual. The family denies neither option.

# A026 stage 1 — good-coordinate erasure

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Universal target: one finite K such that every nonconstant Boolean sign function on every finite uniform cube has some relevant physical i with B_i=H(S_i|S_-i)<=K I_i. H is in bits and I_i=Pr[f(x)!=f(x^i)]. The universal target stays OPEN. Locally closed target: affine Fourier support dimension <=4, and arbitrary disjoint products of these factors, with K=7/2 and H<=(7/2+2/ln2)I. This is an existential coordinate theorem with a hereditary structural hypothesis; no all-coordinate theorem is asserted. Amplitude A_i here means 2 sum|parent adjacent coefficients|, not the Hellinger affinity denoted A_i in the recovered source.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

Full restricted derivation is GOOD_COORDINATE_TARGET.tex. Binary entropy <=2 sqrt(p(1-p)) yields B_i<=A_i. Two exact exhaustive implementations prove selectable A_i/I_i<=7/2 through four variables. If support lies in c+V and relevant e_i is outside V, adjacent Fourier supports are disjoint, B_i=JS=Delta_iH=0. Otherwise all relevant unit vectors lie in V, so at most dim(V)<=4 variables are relevant. Projection under physical restriction preserves the affine-dimension hypothesis. Disjoint independent spectra preserve individual B_i and I_i. Exact decrement and influence identities then telescope/induct with 7/2+2/ln2. The mismatch minority law and KL rebate are retained explicitly; they vanish at zero-cost splits. ## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

B_i(F)<=delta B_i(h)+gamma^2 B_z(A+zD)+delta gamma^2[log2(1/gamma^2)+kappa], kappa=log2(32/27).

The last bracket costs at most C0 I_i(F), C0=kappa+2/(e ln2). No mean/balance hypothesis is imposed on h or the physical rest. The result transfers the signed biased collision to actual uniform Boolean-shadow erasure; it does not pay that erasure or establish coordinate selection. All entropy is in bits. ## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

PROOF.md sections 1-5 give the complete analytic argument. The sharp scalar result is Phi((a+b)^2,c^2)<=Phi(a^2,c^2)+Phi(b^2,c^2)+kappa c^2 for all real amplitudes. After scaling, the derivative of L(x)=Phi_nat(x^2,1) has one turning point. Every positive interior maximum of L(x+y)-L(x)-L(y) must have x=y. Along that diagonal, the maximum occurs at x^2=1/8 and is exactly ln(32/27), establishing the continuum bound and sharpness separately from the finite grid.

Apply it row by row with a=Ahat, b=mu Dhat, c=gamma Dhat. The entire sum of Phi(mu^2 Dhat^2,gamma^2 Dhat^2) cancels the exact negative empty-row subtraction delta Phi(mu^2,gamma^2). The remaining unshifted collision is bounded by gamma^2 B_z(shadow)+delta gamma^2 log2(1/gamma^2), using an elementary scalar derivative argument. This proves the stated arbitrary-bias interface. The Boolean singleton domination |gamma|<=d pays the residual by C0 physical influence. Signed corrections are preserved; they are not replaced by an unsigned gate charge before cancellation.

On any represented actual stopping tree, inherited encoder erasure E_inner and uniform-shadow charges Q_shadow remain explicit. The paid result is H(root)<=E H(leaf)+E_inner+Q_shadow+(C0+2/ln2)spent influence when every node is represented. The exact decrement identity Delta_i H=B_i+J_i and the established JS/influence telescope are consumed, not rediscovered as new results. A003/A004's accepted once-slice lemma can pay E_inner at those nodes by twice their physical influence.

For an encoder-only prefix with a fixed outer shadow, mean-martingale conservation proves the exact ownership Q_shadow=B_z(shadow)[Var(h_root)-E Var(h_leaf)]. Complete encoder trees therefore have the same charge regardless of query policy. A canonical quadratic-bent one-point-flip shadow has selector influence delta=2^-k and B_z>=delta(k-1), analytically for every even k>=4. With a fixed balanced Maj3 encoder, every complete encoder-only policy has Q_shadow/spent encoder influence>=2(k-1)/3. This refutes a dimension-independent payment of this nonnegative bank using encoder influence alone. It is neither an FEI disproof nor a no-go for choosing actual outer-data coordinates; their physical influence is outside that false reserve.

The exact first unsupported universal line is a terminal-inclusive payment of E_inner+Q_shadow under a constructive selection policy with one physical influence budget, including actual outer-data queries. Auxiliary shadow z is not a physical query of F, and unchanged shadow entropy cannot telescope over encoder queries. The AND2-shadow/AND2-encoder control certifies this distinction. A representation using a single physical bit as h simply transfers the original erasure into Q_shadow; the interface itself supplies no universal selection theorem.

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

# A028 stopping-tree payment

Full FEI remains OPEN. This run proves a finite unbalanced-activation stopping ledger and independently reconstructs four existing B06 claims at their stated scopes. The new claim awaits independent review; registry adoption and supervisor copying are not asserted.

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

All physical inputs are independent uniform signs; Fourier coefficients use the normalized Walsh basis; entropy and KL are in bits. Let g be ANY finite-dimensional Boolean sign, delta=Pr(g=-1), r>=1, p=2^-r, and A_r the indicator of one assignment of r fresh physical controls. Put F=1+A_r(g-1). The control-first tree queries controls in a fixed order, stops at the first failure, and otherwise reaches g with probability p.

At a split let a,b be the actual child Fourier coefficients, q_T=(a_T^2+b_T^2)/2, m_T=(|a_T|-|b_T|)^2/4, mu=sum m_T, and nu=m/mu when mu>0. Define the complete nonnegative logarithmic bill J=sum m_T log2(q_T/m_T)=mu[log2(1/mu)-D_2(nu||q)], with zero terms omitted. Write c=log2(e). The recovered split upper bound is Delta H<=J+3c mu. We prove

    E sum J <= (3+c/e) Q,
    E sum (J+3c mu) <= kappa Q,
    Q=2rp delta,  kappa=3+c(3+1/e)<8.

Therefore if g has an FEI-certified terminal allowance K I(g), the FULL expected upper path bill plus terminal allowance is at most max(kappa,K) I(F). This is uniform in dimension, r, delta and the spectrum of g. It pays the mismatch/KL bill itself, not merely the smaller exact entropy decrement.

The same constant holds for every finite expression built from certified terminals by fresh point activations, sign negations, and products on disjoint physical supports, with arbitrary expression depth and control arities. Fix k0,M0 for bounded-overlap terminals; their constants must not be treated as uniform when these parameters grow. Terminal products are certified by exact disjoint additivity; for an activation-only expression the reached leaves are the original recovered terminal or constants.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### 1. Actual control-node packets, including the sign-changing last child

At the active node with s controls left, let u=2^-s. The children are constant +1 and 1+A_(s-1)(g-1). The second child's mean is b0=1-4u delta. All its nonempty coefficients have zero matching coefficient in the constant child. Hence q0=(1+b0^2)/2 and m0=(1-|b0|)^2/4, while m_T=q_T/2 off the empty packet. Parseval gives

    mu=(1-|b0|)/2, m0=mu^2,
    J_s=mu-mu^2+mu^2 log2(q0/mu^2).

For s>=2, b0>=0 even at delta=1; thus mu_s=2u delta. For s=1, mu_1=min(delta,1-delta). The latter is essential: signed disagreement would be delta and is generally NOT the magnitude mismatch. At delta=0 or 1, expressions with zero masses are interpreted continuously.

The node has physical reach w_s=p/u. Its queried influence is d_s=2u delta, including s=1. Thus sum_s w_s d_s=2rp delta=Q and sum_s w_s mu_s<=Q. Physical data influence is p I(g), so I(F)=Q+pI(g) exactly. Every payment below is drawn from Q; pI(g) is reserved for the terminal.

### 2. Pay the complete logarithmic bill

Since q0<=1, for s>=2,

    J_s <= 2u delta+8u^2 delta^2 log2(1/(2u delta)).

For delta>0 write L=log2(1/(2delta)). Each summand 2^-s(s+L) is nonnegative for s>=2 and delta<=1. Therefore its finite sum is bounded by the infinite sum, even when L<0. Using sum_(s>=2)2^-s=1/2 and sum_(s>=2)s2^-s=3/2 gives

    sum_(s=2..r) w_s J_s
    <=2p delta[(r-1)+6delta+2delta L]
    <=2p delta(r+3).

The scalar inequality 6delta+2delta log2(1/(2delta))<=4 on [0,1] follows because its derivative is 6+2L-2c>=4-2c>0 and its value at 1 is 4. Here c<2 follows from ln2>1/2. The delta=0 limit is zero.

At the last node, set a=min(delta,1-delta)<=delta. The preceding packet formula and the scalar maximum x log2(1/x)<=c/e yield

    J_1 <= a+2a^2 log2(1/a) <= (1+2c/e)delta.

If r>=2, w_1=2p and these estimates give

    J_tree <=2p delta[r+4+2c/e]
           <=(3+c/e) Q.

The last inequality uses r>=2. If r=1, J_tree<= (1+2c/e)Q <=(3+c/e)Q, because c/e<2. Adding the full linear allowance 3c sum w_s mu_s<=3c Q proves kappa=3+c(3+1/e).

The strict bound kappa<8 is rationally certified: e>8/3 and ln2>=56/81 imply (3+1/e)/ln2 < (27/8)(81/56)=2187/448<5. Thus kappa<3+5=8. No floating approximation supplies a sign in this argument.

### 3. Exact entropy and terminal ledger

Independently, with eta(t)=t log2(1/t), m=1-2delta and v=4delta(1-delta), the spectrum gives

    H(F)-pH(g)
    =eta((1-2pdelta)^2)-p eta(m^2)
     +4p(1-p)delta^2 log2(1/(4p^2delta^2))+2rpv.

This expression includes the exceptional empty-control/data coefficient and ALL terminal entropy. The exact path costs telescope to this value; the upper mismatch bill bounds it by kappa Q. For terminal allowance K I(g),

    E sum(J+3c mu)+p K I(g)
    <=kappa Q+K pI(g)<=max(kappa,K) I(F).

Inactive leaves are constants with zero influence and entropy. Nothing assumes an FEI bound on arbitrary g: the terminal K is an explicit hypothesis.

### 4. Arbitrary depth without spending influence twice

Given a syntactic expression with fresh activation controls and physically disjoint product factors, construct its tree recursively. At an activation expose controls, stop on failure, and on success execute the core tree. At a product execute each factor's tree in any fixed order. If a residual factor is constant, its sign only changes the residual product's global output sign. For independent factors, squared spectra tensorize; H and I add, and all split q,m,J are unchanged by multiplication by an unqueried disjoint Boolean factor, since its Fourier squared masses sum to one. Thus factorwise path bills add as well. Global output negation leaves these quantities unchanged.

By induction over the expression, every activation pays its newly queried controls and passes its reserved weighted core influence to the core tree. Each product sums physically disjoint budgets. The expression tree is finite and each queried physical coordinate is used at most once along a path. At the stopping leaves, the remaining function is a product of the named certified terminals on disjoint cubes, with the sum of their valid FEI allowances. The constant is the maximum of kappa and their constants, independent of activation depth, arity, bias and number of factors.

For regular terminals take C_reg=log2(4+e)+2log2(e*pi)>8; for bounded-overlap terminals use 41+7log2(k0)+(29/5)log2(M0)>8; for uniform threshold/parity formula terminals use 744. The control closure preserves each of these constants. These are source-reported terminal theorems used conditionally on their recovered acceptance; this run does not re-audit their full proofs. The affine-dimension-four terminal theorem reviewed here has constant 7/2+2/ln2<8, so its activation closure has constant kappa<8.

First unsupported universal line: every arbitrary Boolean root admits such a finite fresh-activation/disjoint-product decomposition into certified terminals, or a different cheap physical tree reaching such terminals. No such representation theorem is proved. Arbitrary cores admitted as leaves would leave pH(g) unpaid and preserve full-FEI hardness via tensor amplification. Shared-input products and general signed multiplexer cells are excluded by actual hypothesis failures, not assumed to satisfy this lemma.

# A028 stage 2: arbitrary control indicators

Full FEI remains OPEN. The B06 lead assigned arbitrary control indicators to A028 and proper partial-core exposure to A027. This stage follows that division and extends the existing A028 stopping-payment candidate with an exact bias correction, a uniform controller-certificate bound, and an actual Boolean obstruction to correction coefficient four. No novelty claim or independent acceptance of this extension is asserted.

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

For every finite uniform physical control cube y and DISJOINT finite uniform core cube x, let c(y) be any Boolean indicator, a(y)=1-2c(y) its sign encoding, and u(x) any Boolean sign. Define

    p=Pr(a=-1), delta=Pr(u=-1), V_a=4p(1-p), V_u=4delta(1-delta),
    F(x,y)=1+c(y)(u(x)-1).

All entropy is in bits; Fourier coefficients are normalized Walsh coefficients. H_+(f) is the unnormalized NONEMPTY contribution sum_(S nonempty) fhat(S)^2 log2(1/fhat(S)^2), not entropy conditioned on nonemptiness. I(a) denotes sign-function total influence; if influence of the 0/1 indicator is instead defined as sum E[(D_i c)^2], it is I(a)/4. This conversion is essential.

The control-first tree exposes physical y coordinates until c is determined, or simply exposes all y coordinates. Its actual weighted leaves are u with probability p and constant +1 otherwise, with irrelevant leftover controls harmless. Let Pi_H=H(F)-pH(u) be the FULL entropy path cost and Q=Pi_I=delta I(a) the queried influence. Reserved terminal influence is pI(u). We prove

    Pi_H <= delta H_+(a)+C0 delta V_a,
    C0=2+2log2(e)+log2(e)/sqrt(e)<6.

This holds for all p,delta, dimensions and actual spectra, including deterministic signs. If H_+(a)<=T[I(a)-V_a], then Pi_H<=max(T,C0)Q. If only H(a)<=K I(a) is known, Pi_H<=(K+C0)Q. Adding a terminal allowance L I(u) gives complete root constants max(T,C0,L), respectively max(K+C0,L). The terminal may itself use a paid finite stopping tree; its reserved influence is multiplied by p exactly once.

Arbitrary repeated variable occurrences INSIDE the representation of c are permitted: c is treated as one actual Boolean function, not a syntactic sum of gate charges. Sharing physical variables BETWEEN c and u, or between separate control modules, is not covered. That sharing changes the actual terminal partition and invalidates pH(u).

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### 1. Exact arbitrary-controller spectrum

Write eta(t)=t log2(1/t), eta(0)=0, and phi(t)=eta((1-2t)^2). For a nonempty core set S and any control set T,

    Fhat(S,T)=uhat(S) chat(T),
    chat(empty)=p, chat(T)=-ahat(T)/2 for T nonempty.

For empty S and nonempty T, Fhat(empty,T)=delta ahat(T). The global empty coefficient is 1-2pdelta. Parseval for the indicator gives sum chat(T)^2=p, and its raw spectral entropy is

    sum_T eta(chat(T)^2)=eta(p^2)+H_+(a)/4+V_a/2.

Summing the entropy of all NONEMPTY core rows and the empty-core/nonempty-control row yields the exact identity

    H_+(F)=delta H_+(a)+pH_+(u)+G(p,delta),
    G=V_u eta(p^2)+V_u V_a/2+delta^2 V_a log2(1/delta^2).

The last term is zero at delta=0 and 1 by continuity. Thus

    Pi_H=delta H_+(a)+G+phi(pdelta)-p phi(delta).

This keeps both exceptional empty coefficients, rather than deleting the negative terminal subtraction. At p=1 it cancels to Pi_H=0; at delta=1 it gives Pi_H=H(a). These endpoint checks are also exact finite certificate checks.

Physical edge counting gives I_x(F)=pI(u), I_y(F)=delta I(a), I(F)=pI(u)+delta I(a). These are dedicated disjoint budgets. The weighted sum of actual queried control influences is Q for every control-only tree determining c; its remaining entropy is exactly pH(u). This uses the already established physical-tree telescope, not a new telescope claim.

### 2. A global bias correction without an endpoint logarithmic loss

Set b=log2(e). The function phi is C^1 on [0,1]. Away from t=1/2,

    phi''(t)=-16log2|1-2t|-24b >= -24b.

The positive logarithmic singularity at 1/2 is locally integrable and phi' is continuous there. Therefore phi(t)+12bt^2 is convex on the whole interval. Apply the convex chord between 0 and delta at pdelta, with phi(0)=0, to get

    phi(pdelta)-p phi(delta)<=12b p(1-p)delta^2=3b delta^2 V_a.

Also -ln p<=(1-p)/p gives eta(p^2)<=b V_a/2, including p=0,1 by continuity. For delta>0, divide the remaining G bound by delta V_a when V_a>0. The result is

    [G+phi(pdelta)-p phi(delta)]/(delta V_a)
    <=2b(1-delta)+2(1-delta)+3bdelta+2delta log2(1/delta)
    =2+2b+delta[b-2+2log2(1/delta)].

The scalar final term has maximum b/sqrt(e), attained at delta=1/(2sqrt(e)): differentiation gives 2log2(1/delta)-b-2=0. The continuous delta=0 limit is zero. This proves the advertised C0. The cases delta V_a=0 are obtained directly from the exact identity, so no illegal division occurs.

The inequality C0<6 is an exact elementary certificate: ln2>=56/81 and e>8/3>(8/5)^2 imply b<=81/56 and 1/sqrt(e)<5/8. Consequently C0<2+(81/56)(21/8)=2597/448<6. These inequalities are rational; no floating sign is used.

Since V_a<=I(a), H_+(a)<=T[I(a)-V_a] gives

    Pi_H<=delta T[I(a)-V_a]+C0delta V_a<=max(T,C0)Q.

Ordinary H(a)<=K I(a), together with H_+(a)<=H(a), gives (K+C0)Q. The actual terminal H(u)<=L I(u) costs pL I(u). Therefore the complete expected path PLUS terminal bound is the stated maximum against Q+pI(u)=I(F), with no reused ancestral influence.

### 3. What this does and does not say about mismatch upper bills

Pi_H is the full ACTUAL entropy decrement, the leaf-partition quantity required by B06 R2. It is not equal to the particular retained magnitude-mismatch upper bill from stage 1. At every physical query the retained KL/log charge J_v satisfies J_v<=Delta_v H. Thus sum w_v J_v<=Pi_H; adding its upper allowance 3b sum w_v mu_v costs at most an additional 3bQ. If a consumer insists on that upper bill, a valid controller-strong rate is max(T,C0)+3b, not silently max(T,C0). The stage-1 point-control bound on that particular upper bill remains unchanged.

### 4. Strong composition and arbitrary finite nesting

Let D=4pdelta(1-p)(1-delta). The physical deficit has the exact nonreuse decomposition

    I(F)-Var(F)=delta[I(a)-V_a]+p[I(u)-V_u]+D.

When D>0, the previously computed correction obeys

    G/D=2+2p log2(1/p)/(1-p)+2delta log2(1/delta)/(1-delta)
        <=alpha, alpha=2+4b<8.

The boundary cases have G=D=0 where required and follow by continuity. Thus strengthened controller/core certificates T_a,T_u compose with constant max(T_a,T_u,alpha). This is the standard disjoint FEI+ composition mechanism specialized to a negative-event conjunction, now stated with an explicit scalar gate correction and the physical stopping budgets. The scalar alpha is sharp for G/D: take actual Boolean a and u equal -1 except at one point on separate r-bit cubes, so p=delta=1-2^-r and the ratio tends to 2+4b. This is sharpness of a correction functional, not an optimal FEI constant or a disproof family.

For a strengthened constant at least b, H_+(f)<=T[I(f)-Var(f)] also implies H(f)<=TI(f), since eta((Ef)^2)<=b Var(f). Therefore finite disjoint conjunction/De Morgan expression trees inherit the indicated complete terminal-inclusive constant, with no dependence on depth. There is no claim for shared physical support.

More directly, if EACH control module has an ordinary certificate K0 and an innermost core has terminal allowance L, sequential fresh activation trees have complete constant max(K0+C0,L), independent of the number of modules. At each new activation the queried module uses Q and passes p times the existing core tree's full budget forward. Intermediate cores need not remain regular. For regular or character-regular controller and terminal modules use K0=L=C_reg, giving C_reg+C0<14.74. This is a concrete conditional application of recovered controller certificates, not a new claim that arbitrary control indicators are regular.

### 5. Point-control strengthening and a certified obstruction

For the point sign a=1-2A_r, p=2^-r, I(a)=2rp, Var(a)=4p(1-p), H_+(a)=8p(1-p)(r-1). For r=1 both H_+ and I-Var vanish. For r>=2 the bound H_+(a)<=6[I(a)-Var(a)] reduces to

    2r-8+4p(r+2)>=0.

At r=2 it is equality, at r=3 it is positive, and for r>=4 it is immediate. Consequently the arbitrary-controller theorem proves Pi_H<=6Q for EVERY unbalanced point-activated core. This improves the ACTUAL leaf-partition rate while retaining the different stage-1 mismatch-bill result.

A tax-free controller charge and correction coefficient four both fail on an ACTUAL three-bit function: take a dictator controller (p=1/2, H_+(a)=H(a)=0, V_a=I(a)=1) and core u=D_2 (delta=1/4). The root is D_3 and terminals are D_2 or +1. Direct integer spectra give

    Pi_H=3-(9/8)log2(3), Q=1/4,
    Pi_H/Q=12-(9/2)log2(3)>4.

The strict sign is the integer certificate 3^9=19683<65536=2^16. It follows that the best universal additive coefficient in Pi_H<=delta H_+(a)+Cdelta V_a is at least this exact ratio, whereas the proof gives C<=C0<6. No optimal coefficient claim is made. The finite witness does not refute FEI.

### 6. Honest universal frontier

Setting u identically -1 gives F=a, Pi_H=H(a), Q=I(a). Hence a fixed finite K paying control-first Pi_H for EVERY controller implies full FEI with the SAME K. Conversely full FEI C and the proved bias bound give a universal control-first rate C+C0. Thus this arbitrary-controller endpoint is FEI-equivalent in finiteness and cannot be declared solved by merely transferring its unpaid H_+(a).

The first unsupported line toward full FEI is a universal controller certificate, or a new structurally justified decomposition/ownership law for overlapping controllers that pays their full controller entropy and terminal cores. Allowing c and u to share inputs invalidates the exact leaf cost pH(u); no overlap extension is asserted. Further fresh nesting on one unchanged core merely combines control indicators and is not a new universal mechanism.

# A029 / B06: actual child-deficit transport and depth-two gain

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

All functions are actual Boolean signs on the uniform cube; H uses bits,
I is total ordinary physical influence, and D_K=KI-H. Full FEI remains OPEN.
The B06 lead's v1 direction replaces rederivation of its R2 telescope with
a bounded measurement of nonlocal gain. For nonconstant f define

  r1(f)=min_{i:Inf_i>0} [H(f)-average H(f_i)]/Inf_i(f),
  r2(f)=min_{T:1<=depth(T)<=2,Pi_I>0} Pi_H(T)/Pi_I(T).

The root must query a relevant coordinate. Each actual physical child may
stop or query a remaining relevant coordinate, independently. The second
query may differ by branch. Root-only trees are included. Pi_H is root
entropy minus weighted terminal entropy; Pi_I is root influence minus
weighted terminal influence. Constants have no divided ratio.

**Certified finite result:** for every nonconstant f in dimensions 1..4,
0<=r1-r2<=2. The maximum is exactly 2. Equality holds precisely for
(a) functions with two essential variables that are signed input/output
variants of AND2, and (b) four-variable bent functions. Here bent means
all 16 squared Fourier coefficients are 1/16. There are 976 equality
truth tables across the enumerated dimensions: 8, 24, 48 AND2 extensions
in dimensions 2,3,4 respectively, and 896 essential four-variable bent
functions. This finite result is not extrapolated to arbitrary n.

**Analytic scoped result:** for the original B05 sparse activated bent
point-surgery family with even k>=4, r=2k, delta=2^-k, p=2^-r,
both physical selector children satisfy D_4>=p(k-3), and the selector
parent satisfies

  D_4(f)>=p[k-3-delta(3k+1/2)]>0.

This explicitly pays an unbounded local-selector ratio from proved child
slack at K=4. No universal induction hypothesis is used for these children.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### What the largest gap means analytically

Take g to be a signed AND of two variables. Its four squared Fourier
coefficients are 1/4, so H(g)=2 and I(g)=1; both influences are 1/2.
Each coordinate restriction is one constant and one signed dictator,
both of entropy zero. Thus Delta_i H=2 and r1=4. Query one coordinate,
then query the dictator child and stop the constant child. Pi_H=2 and
Pi_I=1, so r2=2. No proper tree on these two variables can consume more
than all influence or erase more than all entropy; the root-only ratio
is 4 and the complete tree ratio is 2.

At K=2, the root debt Delta_i H-K Inf_i equals 1. Actual child deficits
are 0 and 2, so the weighted reserve is exactly 1. Revealing the dictator
uses its influence but produces zero entropy drop, creating credit 2
at child reach probability 1/2. All terminal deficits are zero. The
credit is spent once at the parent; it is not also retained at a leaf.

A genuinely four-essential-variable equality witness is truth mask 854
in the stated least-significant-coordinate convention. Root coordinate
0 has child masks 30 and 17. Up to signs they are zg and g on the
remaining variables, with H=2 in both branches and I=2 and 1. The root
has flat spectrum, H=4, I=2, Delta_0 H=2 and Inf_0=1/2. Query the
independent character z in the zg child and stop the other child:
Pi_H=2, Pi_I=1. The terminal entropy is still 2! At K=2 the children
have deficits 2 and 0, paying root debt 1 exactly. This exhibits the
entropy-preserving child query that makes nonlocal payment possible.

Every essential n=4 equality table is a quadratic Boolean sign with a
nonsingular alternating 4x4 adjacency matrix. The classification script
checks this directly by Boolean Mobius transform and direct Walsh sums,
and reconstructs the complete set from 28 nonsingular graphs times 32
affine signs. The four graph degree multisets are matching (1,1,1,1),
path (1,1,2,2), triangle with attached leaf (1,2,2,3), and complete
graph (3,3,3,3). For any quadratic, squaring the Walsh transform shows
that its Fourier support is an affine translate of the image of its
adjacency matrix, with equal masses; hence entropy equals matrix rank.
For completeness: the autocorrelation at shift t is zero unless At=0,
and otherwise is a sign character on ker(A). Fourier inversion of that
character gives the stated affine flat support of size 2^rank(A).

In the first three graph types, query the neighbor of a leaf vertex.
Both three-variable children have rank 2, hence H=2; the leaf coordinate
is isolated and its derivative is constant, with influence 1 in one
child and 0 in the other. Query it only in the influence-1 child.
The resulting entropy cost is 2 and influence cost is 1. For the
complete graph, each child is a rank-2 triangle; query any child
coordinate in both branches. Each two-variable leaf retains a quadratic
edge and H=2, while queried child influence is 1/2. Again the costs
are 2 and 1. Every root has local ratio 4.

These trees are optimal analytically too. After any root query, a
rank-2 three-variable child graph is either one edge plus an isolated
vertex, a two-edge path, or a triangle. A second query that drops
entropy has (Delta H,Inf)=(2,1/2). Queries preserving entropy have
influence 1/2, except an isolated vertex which has influence 0 or 1.
For that isolated case the two sibling influences are complementary,
so the weighted influence Z of all entropy-preserving second queries
is at most 1/2. Let E be weighted influence of the entropy-dropping
second queries. Then

  Pi_H=2+4E, Pi_I=1/2+Z+E<=1+E,
  Pi_H/Pi_I>=(2+4E)/(1+E)>=2.

Thus the gain 2 for all 896 bent functions is explained without relying
only on numerical pattern recognition. The finite census excludes every
other equality class and every larger gap.

### Actual-child transport on B05

The complete proof is saved immediately in B05_TRANSPORT.md. Here are
the steps needed to audit the budget. For F=1+A(u-1), mu=Eu in [0,1],
c=1-mu, its constant coefficient is 1-pc; nonempty-control/empty-data
coefficients have magnitude pc; each nonempty-data coefficient p uhat(T)
is repeated 2^r times. With h(t)=-t log2 t, the exact entropy is

  H(F)=p[H(u)-h(mu^2)]+2rp[(1-mu^2)+(1-p)c^2]
       +p(1-p)h(c^2)+h((1-pc)^2).

Using h(t)<=(1-t)/ln2, (1-mu^2)+c^2=2c and the actual activation
influence I(F)=p[I(u)+rc] gives

  H(F)<=pH(u)+4rpc+2p/ln2,
  D_4(F)>=p[4I(u)-H(u)-2/ln2].

For the actual b,b' cores, Eu=sqrt(delta) or sqrt(delta)-2delta,
I(u)=k/2 or k/2+2k delta. Both means lie in [0,1]. The elementary
dimensional entropy bound H(u)<=k gives D_4(G),D_4(H)>=p(k-3), using
ln2>2/3. These are proved child facts, not assumptions of full FEI.

Selector influence is d=p delta=2^-3k. By the baseline split identity,
conditioning and the scalar JS bound,

  Delta_y H<=h2(d)+(2/ln2)d<=d(3k+3/ln2),
  Delta_y H-4d<=p delta(3k+1/2).

Since 2^-k(3k+1/2) decreases for k>=4 and equals 25/32 at k=4,
the averaged child reserve is larger than the selector debt. B06 R2
then gives the stated strictly positive lower bound on D_4(f).
This succeeds on the original all-coordinate obstruction while keeping
the whole child entropy in the ledger. It does not pay arbitrary cores.

### Nonreuse and the exact remaining universal gap

Use B06 R2 as the accounting interface, with b_v=K Inf_{i_v}-Delta_{i_v}H.
For every physical cut L, D_K(root)=sum_{rho in L} w_rho D_K(f_rho)
plus the b_v terms strictly above the cut. Expanding a child replaces
its deficit by its descendant cut deficits and increments; it never adds
them to the unexpanded deficit. Finite induction on successive cuts
gives an ownership rule for all transfers: each terminal deficit and
each signed increment has exactly one owner, identified by physical
node and reach probability. Internal reserves may be used as shorthand
only until expanded. Negative terminal deficits must remain signed.

The repeated-slack mistake has an actual Boolean witness: for m-bit
parity, D_K=Km, but summing weighted actual deficits of every internal
node in a complete tree gives K*m*(m+1)/2. All that apparent extra
reserve consists of the same still-unqueried coordinates repeated at
successive cuts. There is no entropy debt in this example, so any such
reserve-summing rule is already invalid on a trivial FEI case.

Disjoint tensors of m signed AND2 factors retain r1=4 and r2=2. A tree
that clears one factor consumes influence 1 and entropy 2, leaving the
other factors intact. More sharply, for F=g_AND2*h on disjoint variables,
the same tree has Pi_H=2, Pi_I=1 and terminal functions equal to signed
h. Thus D_K(F)=D_K(h)+(K-2). The cheap depth-two step does not erase or
prove the seed's entropy bound. A universal proof still needs a physical
tree whose total paid increments plus actual terminal deficits establish
D_K>=0 for every seed, at one dimension-independent K. This is the
first unsupported universal line. No new broad universal candidate is
opened while B06's pending-review queue is capped.

# A029 stage 2: depth-sensitive bent child deficits

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Uniform physical cubes, Boolean signs, entropy in bits, ordinary total
influence. D_K=KI-H. Let r1 be the best relevant single-coordinate
physical entropy-drop/influence ratio, r_d the best nonempty physical
stopping-tree ratio Pi_H/Pi_I with maximum depth d, and r_all allow
arbitrary finite trees. These are different optimization domains.

For F_g(x,y)=(-1)^(x dot y)g(y), every m>=1 and every Boolean m-bit
seed g, r1=4 and r_2=2. For quadratic bent roots, r_all=2. For arbitrary
bent roots the proposed gain bound r1-r_all<=2 is FALSE: a cubic bent
root on six variables has an explicit depth-six tree with
r1-Pi_H/Pi_I=(392-9log2(3))/186>944/465>2.03.
This does not refute the original n<=4 depth-two theorem or full FEI.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

in full; stage-start BOARD.md snapshot 2026-10-12 03:34 UTC;

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

DERIVATION.md contains the full proof saved before computation. Fourier
summation over x gives Fhat(A,B)=2^-m g(A)(-1)^(A dot B), so H(F)=2m,
I(F)=m and every root influence is 1/2. A physical coordinate child
has coefficients 0 or +/-2^(1-m), hence H=2m-2 and every root ratio is4.

For any physical restriction, let P be pairs with both coordinates
free, U those with x fixed and y free, V those with y fixed and x
free, p=|P|, and ell the number of V coordinates with fixed y=1.
For each assignment t to y_P let u_t be the actual Boolean U-core
g(t,y_U,fixed y) times the character determined by fixed x_U.
Partial Fourier summation gives the exact laws

  H(F_rho)=2p+E_t H(u_t),
  I(F_rho)=p+ell+E_t I(u_t),
  D2(F_rho)=2ell+E_t D2(u_t).

The x_P frequency equals t, the y_P frequency is uniform, and the
x_V set is a fixed character. All signs and the actual core law are
retained. For codimension<=2, |U|<=2; every Boolean two-bit function
is a constant, character, or signed AND2, and satisfies H<=2I.
Thus all such leaves have D2>=0. Root D2=0 and R2 imply every
depth-two tree has Pi_H>=2Pi_I. Query y_j and then x_j only on y_j=1
to obtain entropy cost2 and influence cost1, attaining r_2=2.

Choose g=-1 only at 000, with m=3. Then H(g)=4-(9/8)log2(3),
I(g)=3/4, and D2(g)=(9/8)log2(3)-5/2<0. Reveal all x; stop at x=0;
complete y to constants on the other seven x assignments. Its actual
terminal averages are H(g)/8 and I(g)/8=3/32, giving

  Pi_H=6-H(g)/8, Pi_I=93/32,
  Pi_H/Pi_I=(352+9log2(3))/186.

The integer certificate 3^5=243<256=2^8 proves log2(3)<8/5, the
ratio<916/465 and gain>944/465>2.03. Its terminal deficit is
D2(g)/8=-5/16+(9/64)log2(3); the query credit is its negative.
Their sum is exactly zero, preserving the bent root D2=0. Omitting
this negative terminal cost would incorrectly treat the gain as an
entropy bound improvement. Disjoint tensors retain this depth-six
counterexample in dimensions 6t, still with H/I exactly2.

Every quadratic restriction has entropy equal to its adjacency rank r
and at least r nonisolated influence-1/2 variables; isolated character
variables only add influence. Thus D2>=0 in every quadratic restriction.
R2 bounds every tree ratio below by2; complete trees attain2. The
lead's parenthetical "bent 2k: gain exactly2" therefore requires a
quadratic hypothesis for unrestricted trees.

More generally, at a root with D_K=0, all stopping ratios are >=K iff
all proper physical restrictions have D_K>=0: if one is negative,
isolate it and complete all off-path siblings. This is a terminal
ownership consequence of R2, not a new universal payment theorem.
The first unsupported line is a dimension-independent payment after
arbitrary terminal deficits are retained; none is established here.

# A029 stage 3: priority transport and a minimal-depth bent gain counterexample

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Boolean signs on independent fair physical bits; entropy in bits,
ordinary influence; D_K=KI-H. r1 is the best relevant root coordinate
entropy-drop/influence ratio. r_d is the best nonempty physical
stopping-tree ratio Pi_H/Pi_I with maximum depth d; r_all allows
all finite physical stopping trees. The domain distinction is essential.

For any m>=1 and any Boolean seed g on m bits, let
F_g(x,y)=(-1)^(x dot y)g(y), p=2^-m. The priority physical tree
described below satisfies the exact arbitrary-seed formulas

  Pi_I=2-p(2+I(g)), Pi_H=4-p(4+H(g)),
  E D_K(leaf)=(K-2)(m-2+2p)+pD_K(g),
  KPi_I-Pi_H=2(K-2)-p[2(K-2)+D_K(g)]

for every real K. No hypothesis H(g)<=KI(g) is imposed or inferred.

For the three-bit point-defect seed, a seven-node depth-four tree
has Pi_H/Pi_I=(184+9log2(3))/102 and therefore
r1-r_all >= (224-9log2(3))/102>524/255>2.05.
Depth four is the first possible gain>2 for this particular root,
certified by an exact finite stopping-cut DP. No globally optimal
ratio or minimum dimension over all Boolean functions is claimed.

The root is a six-variable bent function with H/I=2. Tensor copies
retain this helper violation uniformly in dimensions6t and still
have H/I=2. Full FEI remains OPEN.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

DERIVATION.md contains the full arbitrary-seed proof and compact tree.
Sum Fourier characters over x to obtain
Fhat(A,B)=2^-m g(A)(-1)^(A dot B). Thus every coefficient square is
2^-2m, H(F)=2m, I(F)=m. For any root physical coordinate both children
have coefficient magnitudes0 or2^(1-m), so H(child)=2m-2 and r1=4.

Priority tree: query x1,x2,... until the first1, at index i. Query
y1,...,yi and stop. Stop at g when all x are0. On a first1 branch,
fixing the matching y coordinates leaves a paired carrier on m-i
pairs with an actual restricted seed and a global sign. Every such
terminal has H=2(m-i), I=m-i, so its D_K=(K-2)(m-i) is an exact
proved fact. The lone g leaf has physical probability p.

The elementary sum sum_i 2^-i(m-i)=m-2+2p gives the terminal averages

  E I=m-2+2p+pI(g), E H=2(m-2+2p)+pH(g).

Subtract these from root I=m,H=2m to obtain the stated Pi_I,Pi_H.
The first root query has influence1/2, ensuring Pi_I>0. Rearranging
gives the two signed deficit accounts for every K. Their sum is
(K-2)m exactly. At K=2, the full leaf deficit is pD2(g) and the
query credit is -pD2(g). Each account is owned once; the seed is not
assumed certified and is not paid again from descendant credits.

Choose g=-1 only at000. Its spectrum is9/16 at the empty set and
1/16 at each nonempty set, so H(g)=4-(9/8)L, I(g)=3/4, L=log2(3).
The actual compact tree, with coordinates x1,x2,x3,y1,y2,y3, is:

1. Query x1. On x1=1, query y1 and stop.
2. On x1=0, query x2. On x2=1, query y2; stop if y2=1;
   if y2=0, query y1 and stop.
3. On x1=x2=0, query x3. Stop if x3=0; otherwise query y3 and stop.

The complete eight-leaf list and exact squared-spectral entropies are
in out/compact.json. It has the g leaf at weight1/8, two four-bit
bent leaves at weights1/4, four two-bit bent leaves of total weight
5/16, and one constant at weight1/16. These leaves give

  E I=45/32, E H=21/8+H(g)/8,
  Pi_I=51/32, Pi_H=23/8+(9/64)L.

The elementary integer certificate 3^5=243<256=2^8 implies L<8/5.
Hence this tree ratio<496/255 and its gain>524/255>2.05. The
terminal deficit is -5/16+(9/64)L<0; the query credit is
5/16-(9/64)L>0. They cancel exactly to root D2=0.

The direct-transform checker also minimizes expected terminal D2
over all physical stopping cuts of maximum depths3 and4. Its
Bellman recursion is min(actual terminal D2, average of child
envelopes), minimizing over every still-free coordinate. Every
comparison is exact equality of prime-log vectors or a strict
rational-interval sign. It proves minimum cut D2=0 at depth3 and
-5/16+(9/64)L at depth4. By R2 at a D2=0 root, negative cut D2
is equivalent to a paid-tree ratio below2. Thus maximum depth4 is
necessary and sufficient for gain>2 on this witness. This is not a
certificate that the selected tree globally minimizes Pi_H/Pi_I.

Stage2 proves depth-two gain2 for every paired seed and every m:
all codimension<=2 leaves have D2>=0 through the unmatched-pair
core law, and a paired query attains ratio2. It also proves arbitrary
tree gain2 on quadratic bent roots. The lead's unrestricted reading
of "bent 2k: gain exactly2" is false on this cubic root; these narrower
statements survive. The n<=4 certificate needs no correction.

The first unsupported universal line is a bound on the complete
terminal-inclusive account for arbitrary roots at one absolute K.
A small Pi_H/Pi_I alone is insufficient: the actual terminal seed
may have D_K<0, even when the bent parent is already FEI2-certified.
We open no new broad universal candidate while the pending-review
queue is capped; this is the lead-requested helper test and its repair.

# A030 / B06: exact restriction plateaux and terminal ratio amplification

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Resolve A030's coded-plateau interface for every Boolean seed g on every finite uniform cube. Outputs are signs; H is Shannon entropy of squared Fourier coefficients in bits; I is total physical influence. Establish exact all-branch bounded-depth entropy preservation, auxiliary influence cost, and what a global stopping partition retains. Per B06 lead v1, also quantify restriction amplification of H/I. This is an interface derivation, not a new broad FEI candidate. Full FEI remains OPEN.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

Read execution protocol revision 2026-10-11-execution-v2 in full; canonical README/status/claims/obstructions/packet/stream-action indices; plan A030 contract at lines 696-700 and B06 section; earlier B05, B09, B19 reports; supplemental CAMPAIGN/CLAIMS/COUNTEREXAMPLES/SEED_LEMMAS/NATIVE_MAPPING and source collection. Read BOARD snapshot 2026-10-11 22:57 UTC and B06 STREAM_NOTES v1 22:38Z before work. Exact observed local SHA256 receipts for 34 files are in out/source_manifest.json.

Mathematical precursors actually inspected: ES section 3, pair lift (8)-(9); ISO sections 4-5, isospectral splice and signed-symmetry peeling; seed ledger C015/X001; point-defect formula in SEED_LEMMAS section 3. The general lift is an extension of the known pair lift, not claimed as prior-art novelty. The new packet output is its quantified all-choice lookahead/terminal interface, the nonunate stopping-depth obstruction, and the unbounded general one-step amplification diagnostic.

Inspected all four local paper text files, introductions and relevant restriction/composition/scope passages. Threshold 38pp (11 October 2026), Proposition 7.1 and Corollary 7.3, section 8.3's fresh-sign tensor balancing, and section 8.4's activation reductions provide scope checks. The earlier 29pp version is not substituted for it. Percolation 30pp section 12 explicitly limits its geometric result; the other 31pp version has different overlap-conditional wording. No geometric theorem, biased composition theorem, or version-dependent percolation assertion is used in the proof. Text extracts are cp1252; exact Fourier algebra below bypasses damaged mathematical glyphs.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

Write f=sum_S a_S chi_S. Fix A to rho. The restricted coefficient at T is sum_{S\A=T} a_S chi_{S cap A}(rho). If the projection is injective on occupied support, this sum has one nonzero term; hence the full positive squared-mass multiset is unchanged, H(f_rho)=H(f), and I(f_rho)=sum_S |S\A|a_S^2=I(f)-sum_{i in A}Inf_i(f). Spectral A-membership is determined by the complement, and all physical assignments have the same labelled complement spectrum: both block erasure and channel information vanish. If support distance is >=L, projection collisions require >=L deleted coordinates. Thus every adaptive tree of depth <=L-1 has zero entropy decrement on every branch. No rare-branch qualification is needed.

Construct F_L=g(y), y_i=product_j x_ij, L>=2. The seed label S maps to the union of its L-coordinate blocks. This injective map multiplies sizes and distances by L, so H(F_L)=H(g), Inf_ij(F_L)=Inf_i(g), I(F_L)=L I(g). While each block retains ell_i>=1 coordinates, the residual has H(g) and I=sum_i ell_i Inf_i(g), independently of assignments. Querying L-1 coordinates per block yields a genuine fair tree of depth n(L-1), with 2^[n(L-1)] equally probable coordinate-sign translates of g as leaves. Hence E H_leaf=H(g), E I_leaf=I(g), Pi_H=0, Pi_I=(L-1)I(g), and leaf/root H/I amplification exactly L when H(g)>0. L=2^k gives iterated pair-expansion amplification 2^k, but undoing it requires n(2^k-1) physical queries, not k singleton queries.

For nonconstant g and L>=d+2, every restriction of <=d coordinates retains at least two inputs in every block. In a relevant block, fix other block parity values witnessing a nonzero seed derivative. The derivative in one physical coordinate equals the product of its companion block inputs times that nonzero seed derivative. Flipping a second input reverses the sign. Thus the residual is nonunate, even after arbitrary coordinate sign reversals. No depth-d physical tree can have only C001-regular leaves. This does not exclude longer trees or C004-certified roots.

For amplification at one singleton step take g_r=1-2 indicator_{all+} and G_r,m=g_r product_j z_j. The fresh parity shifts every Fourier label by the fixed spectator set, so H is unchanged and I=I(g_r)+m. Every spectator restriction removes one unit of influence and changes at most the overall sign; both children have the same H and I. With p=2^-r, the core coefficients are 1-2p at the empty set and -2p otherwise, giving I(g_r)=2rp and H(g_r)=(1-2p)^2 log2(1/(1-2p)^2)+8p(1-p)(r-1). Therefore H(g_r)/I(g_r)->4, while H(G_r,1)/I(G_r,1)->0. The amplification equals 1+m2^(r-1)/r and is unbounded already for m=1 on both probability-1/2 branches. This disproves a universal amplification factor, not FEI. B06 lead's at-most-two factor is valid for C015's particular global lift, not arbitrary restrictions. Repeating that particular global lift on its even-supported output adds no further spectral coordinate. Pair expansion is the correct iterated map.

On an injective restriction of d coordinates with H>0, amplification is exactly 1+sum_{i in A}Inf_i(f)/I(f_rho)<=1+d/I(f_rho). If the residual is balanced, Parseval gives I(f_rho)>=1, so amplification<=d+1. The unbounded example has an unbalanced terminal and therefore does not violate this narrower bound.

Full proof is in RESTRICTION_PLATEAUX.tex. The first unsupported universal inference would be: 'zero paid path entropy, or zero lookahead erasure, certifies terminal H(g)<=K I(g) for arbitrary g.' That is unpaid FEI, not a consequence of these identities. A valid ratio-certified root stopping rule is not refuted; only inheritance of root ratio certification by descendants and bounded-depth C001 admission are excluded.

# A030 stage 2: reserve clipping and order sensitivity on exact plateaux

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

Read updated BOARD (supervisor 2026-10-13 21:14 UTC), B06 STREAM_NOTES v2 (2026-10-11 23:26Z), own STATUS/LEARNED and prior report/claim. ## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

Use Q_C(v)=[D_v-Cd_v+E Q_C(child)]_+, zero at constant leaves. Its least-nonnegative property is backward induction. The optimal stopping representation follows by choosing stop for zero or query and continue; it implies [H-CI]_+<=Q_C<=H. These baseline assertions are reconstructed in REVIEW.md; no new entropy/influence telescope is claimed.

Let q=Q_C(g,T). In an injective compression of F_L=g(product_j x_ij), D=0 at every prefix, each query in block i has d=Inf_i(g), and each compressed core has the same translated continuation reserve q. With ell_i surviving inputs/block and b_v=sum_i(ell_i-1)Inf_i(g), the exact solution is Q_C(v)=[q-Cb_v]_+. The algebra [-Cd+[q-C(b-d)]_+]_+=[q-Cb]_+ holds for C,d>=0. This proves the formula for every adaptive compression order that completes the same partition. The root value is [q-C(L-1)I(g)]_+; m parity spectators give [q-Cm]_+. A zero reserve at the root can therefore precede positive reserve on every branch without any entropy drop.

The alternative whole-block simulation queries L physical inputs whenever T queries a seed bit. Its first L-1 queries have zero entropy decrement; the last has exactly the seed decrement D. Every query in that block has influence d, and the parity outcomes are fair. Combining the nested positive parts yields [D-LCd+E Q_child]_+. Hence Q_C(F_L,block-simulation T)=Q_(LC)(g,T). Increasing a stopped-prefix price from C to LC loses at most C(L-1)I(g), because the prefix's spent influence is at most I(g). Thus [Q_C(g,T)-C(L-1)I(g)]_+<=Q_(LC)(g,T). This comparison is conditional on the specified core tree and does not characterize all policies.

For ordinary addressing A_a, H=2a,I=1+a/2. In data-first T, stopping after all data reveals spends influence1 and leaves a random sign table on a address bits of entropy<=a. Therefore Q_C(A_a,T)>=[a-C]_+. Set C=4,L=2. Compression-first has Q_root=[q-4I(A_a)]_+=0 because q<=2a<2a+4. Whole-block data-first has Q_root=Q_8(A_a,T)>=[a-8]_+. In the latter data-block prefix the actual queried influence is2 and retained expected influence is a; retained entropy is<=a. Both completed trees have Pi_H=2a,Pi_I=a+2. Extending constant leaves with all remaining zero-influence queries makes their physical leaf partition exactly the same set of singleton inputs without changing Q. Their reserve difference therefore grows at least a-8 even with the same completed partition and same telescoped total debt -2a-8. The root H/I=2a/(a+2)<2 throughout. This is an order-sensitive stopped-prefix reserve obstruction, not an FEI counterexample.

For the concrete small A_3 data-first core, the exact recursion gives q=(9/32)log2(3)-3/8. Integer inequalities3^9>2^14 and3^3<2^5 certify1/16<q<3/32. At C=4, z A_3 with spectator-first continuation has Q_root=0 and reserve q on both children. Their H/I are already below4; a positive Q is a constraint on future prefixes rather than a child FEI deficit. For the all-a spectator variant m=ceil(2a/C), C>0, q<=2a implies root Q=0 while q>=[a-C]_+ reappears on every compressed-core branch. Unpaid prefix costs excluding the spectators must not be substituted for the complete root debt, which includes their prepaid influence.

For a general zero-entropy subtree with nonnegative leaf reserves q_l and nonnegative cumulative path cost b_l, [E q_l-C E b_l]_+<=Q_root<=E[q_l-Cb_l]_+. Full continuation or immediate stopping proves the lower bound. Any strategy aborted before a leaf has payoff minus a nonnegative spent cost; complete continuation on a path has payoff q_l-Cb_l. Thus the pathwise positive part bounds every adapted strategy, proving the upper bound. It is an oracle bound and may be strict. An actual example: for a>=13, z A_a at C=4, use data-first on one spectator branch (q>=9) and address-first on the other (reserve0, since each address reveal has D=2,d=1/2). The parent reserve is q/2-4, whereas the oracle upper bound is q/2-2: gap exactly2. These child spectra agree, but their prescribed continuations differ. No claim that Q is a function of the Boolean function alone is made.

A064's terminal-corrected bill P=H(node)-E H(final leaf) is instead invariant through exact compression with translated continuations. Stop at g and P=0, with all H(g) retained at the terminal; continue to constants and P=H(g) throughout the plateau. Its resource bound and the priced minimal Q_C are distinct. The complete proofs and construction details are in RESERVE_TRANSPORT.md.

First unsupported universal line: for arbitrary Boolean roots, pay the actual stopped-prefix envelope or its collision-induced continuation values by one exclusive physical influence reserve, while retaining a certified terminal entropy account. Nothing here bounds that positive-erasure/collision residual universally. B06 R2's additive leaf ledger remains valid; only an extension of it to the nonlinear minimal nonnegative reserve is refuted. A029's proposed bound on entropy-drop/influence ratio gains is not refuted by these absolute reserve gaps.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

where L(q)=phi(q^2)-phi((1-2q)^2)/4+2q(1-q). This identity is exact for arbitrary core spectra. With supplied original C-priced cut potentials, P=(V/4)sum r_j, so P+R pays controller queries at C+14. The exact local identity is D_F-Cd-(P+R)+E(P_child+R_child)=E-s, with s the actual unused cut-transaction slack. A complete control-only tree retains sum q_j H(u_j) in its terminals. No new root bank or core FEI certificate appears.

At labels(0,0,0,0,1,1,1,2), three independent two-bit point defects of common mean1/2, query2 then0,1, and C1: d=3/8, E=-3/8+(27/64)log2(3), s=3/16, and E-s=-9/16+(27/64)log2(3) in(1/10,7/64). # A032 stage2: sharp six-variable two-address endpoint

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

For every uniform Boolean F on six physical coordinates, with a distinguished pair of addresses A such that every positive-mass conditional spectral law of the other four coordinates given the address label is product, decide whether H/I can exceed Cstar=16/3-(3/2)log2(3). Result: it cannot. Exact complete admission and an objective certificate prove H<=Cstar I, and an embedded three-variable point defect attains equality. Coordinate permutation and dummy padding give the same bound for all functions on at most six variables in the class. H is in bits and I is physical total influence. Full FEI and the all-dimensional two-address class remain OPEN.

The common mixed mask M must agree in all four rows; outside M the occupied pure masks partition its complement. The exact XOR of row sign masks on M must be0 or M modulo global row signs. # A032 stage3: exact weighted nonfair-row phase admission

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Fix two physical address bits A and at most four physical data bits. For an actual Boolean F, write g_J(x)=E_y F(x,y)chi_J(y), h_J=2g_J, and w_J=E_x h_J^2/4. Assume every positive conditional squared Fourier law of the data given J is a coordinate product. Let N_J count its nondeterministic nonfair marginals, Q=sum w_J N_J, and I_A=sum w_J |J|. H(w) is in bits. We certify the sharp finite bound Q<=Kstar(H(w)+I_A), Kstar=[45/8-(17 log2(17)+15 log2(5))/32]^-1 approximately0.42280945898152317. The all-dimensional target Q<=K(H(w)+I_A) remains OPEN. This is a narrow refinement of an existing certified domain, not another broad universal candidate.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

Read BOARD.md snapshot2026-10-13 22:05UTC, B07 STREAM_NOTES.md v2 dated2026-10-11 23:45UTC, own STATUS.md and LEARNED.md, and all of EXECUTION_PROTOCOL.json revision2026-10-11-execution-v2. Consumed prior A032 stage2 CLAIMS.json, MIXED_COMPLETENESS.md paragraphs1-6, cover_search.py, mixed_cover.py, certify_full.py and certify_pure.py. The prior catalogue source is out/mixed_slices_m4.json in run20261011T234647Z-account05-A032-boolean-phase-realizability. A034 STATUS.md was read; its newer run directory contains FROZEN_SPEC.json/code but no REPORT.md, so no new result from it is assumed. Prior campaign and paper context remains background; no new external theorem or web source is used. Source hashes are in ARTIFACT_RECEIPTS.json.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

Pointwise address Booleanity has exactly two forms: one h_J=+/-2 and three zeros, or four h_J=+/-1 whose signs have product-1. Thus all rows share M={x:|h_J(x)|=1}; pure masks E_J partition M's complement, and the XOR of negative odd masks must be either0 or M modulo freely chosen global row signs. This is an exact common-phase test.

We reuse the complete45088-row catalogue on four data bits, with its previously recorded primitive ratio/template/gauge completeness proof. For each row an integer Walsh transform W gives z=sum W_s^2, marginal numerator t_i=sum_(s contains i) W_s^2, and N=sum_i 1[t_i not in{0,z} and2t_i!=z]. No floating-point marginal test occurs. All rows have N<=1. In this catalogue N is determined by norm k=sum h^2: N=1 precisely for k in{5,10,20,34,40}; N=0 for k in{1,2,4,8,16,32,64}.

For each nontrivial common mask, first compress rows only by (E, negative-odd-mask, k, N), which preserves admission and the objective. Each disjoint pair is compressed by (pure-union U, odd-sign-XOR R, sorted pair norms, sum kN). Pair lookup on (complement U, R) or (complement U,R xor M) covers every admissible tuple. Crucially the old entropy/data-influence compression is not used: it could lose this new objective. The690 nontrivial masks all complete. Pure tuples are covered separately by first-uncovered-point recursion on disjoint supports, including up to four nonzero rows and zero padding. All-mixed rows are Boolean product-spectrum rows and have N=0 in every dimension: primitive coprime factors satisfy c^2 product(a_i^2+b_i^2)=P^2, a power of two. Opposite-parity primitive pairs contribute an odd factor>1, whereas two odd entries contribute2 modulo8; therefore each active pair is(1,1). This also proves the product-spectrum Boolean tensor guard without a new external dependency.

Address labels can be assigned weights0,1,1,2 to descending masses. Any permutation of four labels preserves the two pointwise admission forms, so it gives a realizing Boolean F. This minimizes I_A and hence suffices for all physical address assignments. There are10 distinct (sorted four masses, Q) signatures including pure configurations; all-mixed Q=0 is disposed of separately. The executed pure recursion records3908 compressed support covers, which is a compressed diagnostic, not a truth-function count. The47024 mixed pair matches are also diagnostic.

For masses k_J/64 and address numerator a=k_1+k_2+2k_3, D=H(w)+I_A=6-sum k log2(k)/64+a/64. The positive-Q maximizing signature is k=(34,10,10,10), Q=1, a=40. Its Dstar=45/8-(17 log2(17)+15 log2(5))/32. For every signature, compare D-Q Dstar exactly as a rational linear combination of prime logarithms. The32-term atanh series after scaling its argument into[1,2] has remainder bounded by 2t^65/[65(1-t^2)], t=(x-1)/(x+1), and ln2 is bounded positively before division. The certificate has8 strictly positive differences and2 exact zero differences (the nonconstant maximizer and zero-cost constant); its interval for Dstar lies above2, proving Kstar<1/2. All comparisons retain exact rational fractions.

The attaining64-entry truth table is in out/witness.json. A direct character-sum transform verifies its Boolean signs, Parseval4096, w=(17,5,5,5)/32, all four N=1 and I_A=5/8. The conditional marginals and full integer spectrum are included; coordinate-product identities are checked exactly. No merely normalized vector is offered as a witness.

The three-bit point defect remains admitted: w=(5,1,1,1)/8, Q=5/8, I_A=1/2, conditional marginals(1/10,1/2,1/2,1/2). A normalized non-Boolean control psi=product_i(sqrt(15)+x_i)/4, independent of the addresses, has one address row of mass1, Q=m and H(w)+I_A=0, and fails Booleanity at all-plus. Thus the tested payment is genuinely dependent on Boolean phase admission. Tensoring with an actual Boolean product-spectrum seed only adds fair/deterministic row marginals and leaves this payment unchanged. Arbitrary Boolean tensors may leave the conditional-product class; that universal guard remains a limitation.

The first unsupported line for a universal conclusion is: the common identity 4h_J^2-h_J^4=3 1_M for all J forces sum w_J N_J <=1/2(H(w)+I_A) independently of the number of data bits. The identity itself follows by substitution of the five allowed values; the payment does not yet follow. Even this payment leaves address entropy and conditional entropy to be paid in a universal FEI argument. H(F)=H(w)+sum w_J sum_i h2(p_Ji) is retained; the elementary bound on the conditional term is <=2I_data+Q, and no missing payment of H(w) is inferred from it.

# A032 stage4: universal affine admission and a cubic necessity certificate

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

For actual Boolean F(y,x), with two physical address bits y and arbitrarily many data bits x, let h_J=2 E_y F chi_J, w_J=E h_J^2/4, N_J count nondeterministic nonfair conditional spectral marginals, and Q=sum w_J N_J. The open target is Q<=K(H(w)+I_A) independent of data dimension, where I_A=sum w_J|J| and entropy is in bits. This stage closes that payment only when every nonzero row has coordinate-product squared spectrum and affine Fourier signs on its coordinate spectral face. Its sharp constant is5/(28-5log2(5)) approximately0.305057371816754. It also gives a universal exact impossibility certificate for quadratic signs on the genuine4:1 heavy row law. Full FEI remains OPEN.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

Read BOARD.md snapshot2026-10-14 01:51UTC, then its exact B07/A079/A088 entries; B07 STREAM_NOTES.md v2 dated2026-10-11 23:45UTC, own STATUS.md, and all EXECUTION_PROTOCOL.json revision2026-10-11-execution-v2. Read A034 STATUS.md and its first-run REPORT.md/PHASE_SCOPE.md: affine character cells are a known special case, quadratic physical-row payment is already scoped, and no arbitrary X-restriction stability is available. Read A079 newest DERIVATION.md (run20261014T000722Z), especially Quadratic Fourier facts, exact overlap, and its terminal/collision gap. ## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

PROOF.md contains the full factor-gcd argument: removing an affine Fourier sign character makes the physical inverse a product of primitive linear factors times a physical character. The gcd of each primitive factor's two values is1 or2; after extracting those gcds, each nonfair factor contributes at least2 to the maximum integer height. Height<=2 permits only a unit subcube indicator, twice that indicator, or a single(2,1) factor times a subcube indicator. In a shared mixed configuration, M is a coordinate subcube of codimension c. Every nonfair row's pure mask is one of only c adjacent subcubes, while all pure masks must cover2^c-1 copies of M. Therefore2^c-1<=c forces c=1, exactly one nonfair row, and w=(5,1,1,1)/8. The sharp payment follows with I_A>=1/2. The actual three-bit defect attains it and remains the vacuous-conditional embedding guard.

The full mixed entropy is retained: H(F)=4-(9/8)log2(3), I_data>=1/4, I_A>=1/2, proving sharp H/I<=Cstar in every data dimension under this admission. Pure affine rows are character cells: H=2 sum w_J codim(cell_J)<=4 I_data. This last estimate is A034's known special case and is included only to complete the terminal ledger. No novelty claim is made for that estimate.

QUADRATIC_OBSTRUCTION.md gives a different universal certificate. For a product-magnitude row with exactly one4:1 ratio and otherwise fair active coordinates, replace that ratio by1:1 to obtain a flat quadratic-phase inverse g. It is constant-modulus u on an affine support L. The desired row would be h=(5g+3T_jg)/2. If L and its translate are disjoint, integer inverse values force u/2 integer, yielding height>=5. If supports coincide, the ratio of the two signs takes both values (otherwise the spectral coordinate j would be deterministic), yielding magnitudes4u andu, with u integer and height>=4. Both contradict the allowed height2. Reversing the ratio orientation changes no conclusion.

The existing genuine Boolean witness has heavy conditional marginal16/17 on data coordinate0 and1/2 on the others, so it fits that exact labeled4:1-law obstruction. Every realizing Fourier sign polynomial for that law has degree at least3. Its actual phase has ANF masks[0,3,5,9,13], so the cubic lower bound is attained. This is stronger than noting four-cycle violations in its current truth table: it rejects EVERY quadratic sign assignment for that labeled magnitude law. It does not claim address masses alone force a4:1 law or cubic phases.

The normalized non-Boolean product psi=product_i(sqrt(15)+x_i)/4 has affine all-plus Fourier signs and is independent of address. Its Q=m but address entropy and influence are both zero. Thus the new payment depends on integer Boolean inverse admission rather than generic product-probability algebra. The pure/mixed forms exclude this control. Arbitrary Boolean tensor seeds may violate the affine sign/product hypotheses; no unrestricted tensor closure is inferred. Full conditional entropy is explicitly retained in the admitted class.

First unsupported line: arbitrary compatible nonaffine row signs obey Q<=1/2(H(w)+I_A) in every data dimension. The shared identity4h_J^2-h_J^4=3 indicator_M is exact but does not establish that payment. The new quadratic obstruction shows that simply stopping at degree2 would exclude an existing Boolean heavy law, so the next mechanism must already allow cubic signs. One spectral coordinate with two quadratic sign branches gives two flat inverse supports with potentially unequal ranks and partial overlap; paying their common-phase compatibility is unpaid. No C018 conclusion follows from the present restricted theorem.

# A034 phase-energy transfer

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units
For actual Boolean signs on disjoint uniform physical cubes X,Y with integer X-degree bound d>=1, retain and pay H(S_Y|S_X) under a proved stable condition that excludes arbitrary fresh-Y Boolean tensor factors. Entropy is in bits. Result: quadratic affine partition rows with distinct X labels satisfy H(S_Y|S_X)=sum alpha(k+r)<=2 I_Y, H(S_X)=sum alpha k, and H(f)=sum alpha(2k+r)<=4 I_Y. This is a restricted theorem, not a full FEI proof. Full FEI remains OPEN.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line
The complete proof is CONDITIONAL_PHASE_THEOREM.tex. On an affine physical cell a+V, the signed square-Walsh identity averages the quadratic polar character over v; it vanishes unless the difference w lies in the polar kernel. The remaining signed sum on that kernel is either its full size or zero. Hence the normalized row spectrum is uniform on an affine coset of dimension k+r. Its entropy is k+r, while every varying coordinate costs half a unit of expected spectral degree and there are at least k+r varying coordinates. Distinct X labels retain each row without coherent collision. The chain rule keeps the whole conditional entropy, rather than charging a marginal twice. Empty labels and zero influence are covered.

Restriction stability is exact for all Y coordinate restrictions, and for X restrictions that keep projected labels distinct. A fresh-Y tensor factor must be a quadratic phase by fixing one occupied physical row point. Conversely, full physical restriction closure of ordinary addressing recovers any Boolean truth table by setting the data bits to that table. Thus demanding all X restrictions creates an FEI-strength endpoint. The first unsupported universal inference is an influence payment after X labels collide or after nonquadratic row phases are admitted. No assertion of universal stability or unrestricted conditional payment is made.

The normalized non-Boolean control psi_N=N^(-1/2)sum_i y_i has conditional entropy log2 N but I_Y=1, violating the constant-two inequality for N>=5. Thus the statement cannot be inferred from normalization and marginal degree alone. The class excludes cubic tensor phases by their unique degree-three binary monomial. It allows quadratic tensor factors, whose entropy is explicitly paid.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

PROOF.md supplies the complete all-dimensional derivation. For a concentrated partial row h of magnitudes1 on an odd-size mixed mask M and2 everywhere else, put P=2^m, mu=|M| and t=sum_M(-1)^|y|. Product spectra force odd integer Walsh magnitudes with primitive coprime odd pairs(a_i,b_i) and gcd scale1. Let D=product a_i b_i and N count nonfair pairs. Full-complement signed autocorrelation gives D|3t, while Parseval and elementary product inequalities give 8P<=9t^2+6mu+1<=(3mu+1)^2 and 3^N<=D<=3|t|. Thus mu=1 is impossible in every data dimension m>=2, regardless of Fourier phases. The three-bit defect m=1 remains admitted.

For arbitrary division of the pure region among the four rows, with the same odd M, put E_J={|h_J|=2} and t_J=sum_EJ(-1)^|y|. The same argument proves D_J|(t+4t_J) for each row and gcd_J D_J|3t, since the E_J partition the complement. This is a common-phase necessary condition beyond the norm-only constraint; it does not imply the desired weighted payment. A five-data-bit norm template with three(1,3) pairs and two fair pairs passes Parseval4000 and odd parity, but D=27 does not divide96, rejecting all phase assignments without a2^32 phase search.

The full ledger remains H(F)=H(w)+sum_J w_J sum_i h2(q_Ji), with the conditional term<=2I_Y+Q. No address entropy is dropped or paid by the new lemma. The honest first unsupported line is Q<=K(H(w)+I_X) for arbitrary compatible phases in every data dimension, especially even mixed masks and shared pure allocations. The odd-mask restriction is not hereditary or dummy-bit stable. Arbitrary Boolean tensors and general nonproduct spectra are still outside this new lemma, so it does not supersede the original stable restricted theorem.

The normalized non-Boolean product control product(3+4y_i)/5 has Q=m and H(w)+I_X=0; its physical all-plus value exceeds1, excluding it by actual Booleanity. Separately, an actual point-defect family with r address bits and one data bit proves no absolute weighted-payment constant works with unbounded r and unbounded X degree: Q=(1-p)^2+p^2, p=2^-r, while H(w)+I_X<=(5r+4)p. This is a scope obstruction, not a fixed-two-address counterexample or FEI disproof.

REVIEW_A032.md independently reconstructs only the quadratic obstruction clause of A032-AFFINE-ADMISSION-QUADRATIC-OBSTRUCTION@1. Its verdict is HOLDS for that clause. Removing the unequal pair makes a quadratic flat inverse g supported on an affine set; restoring it gives h=(5g+3Tg)/2. # A035 signed cancellation countersearch

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Full FEI remains OPEN. This stage follows B07 lead v1: freeze and test the two universal helpers H(f)-Hinf(f)<=K_gap I(f) and Hinf(f)<=K_min I(f), where Hinf=-log2(max_S fhat(S)^2). Inputs are independent uniform cube bits, outputs are +-1, entropy is in bits, and I=sum_S |S| fhat(S)^2. The selected trial constants are K_gap=4 and K_min=2; these were calibration choices, not claims attributed to another agent. Both constants are rigorously refuted by actual Boolean functions. No all-constant failure or FEI disproof is claimed.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

The complete short derivations are in CANCELLATION_FAILURE.tex. For even n the quadratic bent sign has flat squared spectrum, so H=Hinf=n and I=n/2. Every nonzero signed XOR autocorrelation is zero by f^2=1, but its absolute counterpart is one. Thus total unsigned autocorrelation divided by I is 2(2^n-1)/n and diverges. Triangle inequality is valid in the direction |sum terms|<=sum absolute terms; inferring an upper bound for the right-hand side from the vanishing left-hand side reverses the implication. This refutes the unsigned payment, not an unspecified signed weighted target.

For the tribes sign h_{w,m}, let q=2^-w and r=1-q. Its constant amplitude is 1-2r^m. A Fourier set meeting exactly j tribes has squared amplitude 4q^(2j)r^(2(m-j)) and multiplicity binom(m,j)(2^w-1)^j. Its influence is 2wmq r^(m-1). These follow by expanding the product of independent failures and counting pivotal edges. The six-bit h_{2,3} has histogram {25/1024:1,81/1024:9,9/1024:27,1/1024:27}, I=27/16, and Hinf=10-4log2(3)>2I. The last sign is exactly certified by 3^32<2^53. The twelve-bit h_{3,4} gives the stronger necessary K_min >= (512/1029)(22-2log2(353)) >2.524150672274.

The min-gap helper is itself full-FEI strength. Given ANY Boolean h, set g=y product(j=1..t)h(x_j), which is balanced, and F=(1+z)/2+(1-z)g/2. The constant and z amplitudes are 1/2, and each nonzero g amplitude supplies two amplitudes of magnitude |ghat|/2. Hence H(F)=2+tH(h)/2, Hinf(F)=2, and I(F)=1+tI(h)/2. A universal gap bound with constant K gives tH(h)<=K(tI(h)+2); letting t grow gives FEI with the SAME K. Conversely FEI bounds the gap since Hinf>=0. Thus the optimal universal constants coincide. This is an application of the paper's exact activation identity, with its extra min-entropy accounting; it is not an easier new universal payment.

Taking h=h_{2,8} gives I(h)=2187/2048 and certified 4.623452413789<H(h)<4.623452413790. With t=32 the above F is an explicit Boolean sign on 514 variables, I(F)=2315/128, Hinf(F)=2, and H(F)-Hinf(F)-4I(F) lies between 1.631488620635 and 1.631488620636 bits. No huge truth table is needed: Booleanity follows from the formula; the spectrum, influence and entropy follow from exact tensor factorization and the proved selector identity. This disproves K_gap=4 even though all original finite family checks passed it.

For bent-core point surgery, let the bent core have even k, pad to n>=k, and flip the value at a point of core weight r. With N=2^n and b=N/2^(k/2), integer Walsh squared magnitudes are (b-2)^2 with count (2^k+2^(k/2))/2; (b+2)^2 with count (2^k-2^(k/2))/2; and 4 with count N-2^k. Zero magnitudes are omitted, repeated magnitudes merged. Alignment-count difference 2^(k/2) follows from sum_S bhat(S)chi_S(point)=b(point), so the histogram is point-independent. Flipping the point changes a core edge according to the partner bit, yielding I=k/2+2(n-2r)/N. This proves the symmetry reduction, including dummy coordinates and the constant-output endpoint.

Normalized non-Boolean controls are kept separate: psi_n=n^-1/2 sum x_i has H=Hinf=log2 n and I=1, so it defeats constant-2 FMEI but has zero gap. The dyadic normalized phi_t=3/4+[1/(4*2^t)]sum(i=1..7*4^t)x_i has I=7/16 and gap/I=2t+log2 9. It violates Booleanity already on singleton autocorrelations 3/(8*2^t)>0. Dyadic granularity and normalization do not pay either universal target.

The exact first unsupported universal line remains a dimension-independent signed influence payment for H-Hinf (equivalently full FEI), or another B07 entropy term from full Booleanity. There is no proof of any such bound here. Increasing trial constants or checking more dimensions does not supply it.
